# Every materially improved association

Material = pipeline held-out RMS lower by at least 20 Hz and 25%. This is a descriptive threshold, not a statistical significance test.
All RMS values below are Hz. Positive epoch age means before capture; negative means after. Common-position controls use fixed UTC and refit only source frequency offsets on training data.
Geometry coefficients use later orbital states and are diagnostic, not operational corrections. RTN means radial, along-track and cross-track. Predicted-orbit Doppler mismatch is not measured-signal RMS.
